    var info = APPOINTER_DRIVE_MAP[lastName];
    var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(info.folder_name);
    if (folders.hasNext()) {
      var folder = folders.next();
      var fileCount = 0;
      var files = folder.getFiles();
      while (files.hasNext()) { files.next(); fileCount++; if (fileCount > 100) break; }
      Logger.log("✅ " + lastName + " (" + info.email + "): フォルダ「" +
                 info.folder_name + "」発見, ファイル " + fileCount + "+ 件");
    } else {
      Logger.log("❌ " + lastName + " (" + info.email + "): フォルダ「" +
                 info.folder_name + "」未発見");
      Logger.log("   → アポインターに以下を依頼してください:");
      Logger.log("   1) マイドライブに「" + info.folder_name + "」フォルダを作成");
      Logger.log("   2) このフォルダを " + Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() + " に編集者として共有");
      allOk = false;
    }
  }

  Logger.log("");
  Logger.log("送信先: " + APO_AUDIO_FORWARD_TO);
  Logger.log("最大送信添付サイズ: " + (MAIL_ATTACHMENT_MAX_BYTES / 1024 / 1024) + " MB");
  Logger.log("マッチ許容時間: ±" + (TIME_MATCH_TOLERANCE_MS / 60000) + "分");
  Logger.log("1回の処理上限: " + MAX_PROCESS_PER_RUN + "件");

  // トリガー確認
  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  var hasTrigger = false;
  triggers.forEach(function(t) {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === "forwardApoRecordings") {
      hasTrigger = true;
      Logger.log("✅ forwardApoRecordings トリガー設定済み");
    }
  });
  if (!hasTrigger) {
    Logger.log("⚠️ forwardApoRecordings のトリガー未設定");
    Logger.log("   → 「トリガー」→「+トリガーを追加」→ 関数: forwardApoRecordings");
    Logger.log("     時間主導型 / 5分おき で設定してください");
    allOk = false;
  }

  Logger.log("");
  Logger.log(allOk ? "🎉 全項目OK - 本番稼働可能です" : "⚠️ 上記の項目を修正してください");
  return allOk;
}

// ==========================================================================
// 手動テスト: 過去24時間のアポ録音を一括処理
// ==========================================================================
function testForwardApoRecordings() {
  Logger.log("=== テスト実行: forwardApoRecordings ===");
  var result = forwardApoRecordings();
  Logger.log("テスト結果: " + JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  return result;
}


// ============================================================
// v67: 経営管理システム連携（build_kanri_system.py が取得）
// ============================================================
var KANRI_KEY = "sukima-kanri-2026";  // 経営システム側の設定と一致させる（変更可）

function kanriDateKey_(d){
  if (!d) return "";
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(d) === "[object Date]" && !isNaN(d))
    return Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Tokyo", "yyyy-MM-dd");
  var s = String(d).trim();
  var m = s.match(/^(\d{4})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})/);
  if (m) return m[1]+"-"+("0"+m[2]).slice(-2)+"-"+("0"+m[3]).slice(-2);
  return "";
}
function kanriHM_(v){
  if (v===null || v===undefined || v==="") return "";
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]" && !isNaN(v))
    return Utilities.formatDate(v, "Asia/Tokyo", "HH:mm");
  var s = String(v).trim(); var m = s.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return m ? ("0"+m[1]).slice(-2)+":"+m[2] : "";
}
function kanriMin_(hm){ if(!hm) return null; var p=hm.split(":"); return (+p[0])*60+(+p[1]); }

// 純粋集計関数（Node単体テスト可能：Utilities非依存の整形済み値でも動く）
function kanriAggregate_(att, hist, apo, fromKey){
  var days={}; // key = name + "|" + date
  function slot(nm, dk){
    var k=nm+"|"+dk;
    if(!days[k]) days[k]={name:nm, d:dk, hours:null, est:false, calls:0, connects:0, appos:0, tmin:null, tmax:null};
    return days[k];
  }
  for (var i=1;i<att.length;i++){
    var nm=String(att[i][0]||"").trim(); var dk=kanriDateKey_(att[i][1]);
    if(!nm||!dk||dk<fromKey) continue;
    var ci=kanriMin_(kanriHM_(att[i][2])), co=kanriMin_(kanriHM_(att[i][3]));
    var bs=kanriMin_(kanriHM_(att[i][4])), be=kanriMin_(kanriHM_(att[i][5]));
    if(ci!==null && co!==null && co>ci){
      var brk=(bs!==null&&be!==null&&be>bs)?(be-bs):0;
      slot(nm,dk).hours=Math.round(((co-ci-brk)/60)*100)/100;
    }
  }
  for (var j=1;j<hist.length;j++){
    var hn=String(hist[j][0]||"").trim(); var hd=kanriDateKey_(hist[j][1]);
    if(!hn||!hd||hd<fromKey) continue;
    var s=slot(hn,hd); s.calls++;
    var rid=mapResultLabelToId(hist[j][6]);
    if(rid!=="absent" && rid!=="wrong") s.connects++;
    if(rid==="appo") s.appos++;
    var tm=kanriMin_(kanriHM_(hist[j][2]));
    if(tm!==null){ if(s.tmin===null||tm<s.tmin)s.tmin=tm; if(s.tmax===null||tm>s.tmax)s.tmax=tm; }
  }
  var out=[];
  for (var k in days){
    var v=days[k];
    if(v.hours===null && v.tmin!==null && v.tmax!==null && v.tmax>v.tmin){
      v.hours=Math.round(((v.tmax-v.tmin)/60)*100)/100; v.est=true;
    }
    out.push({d:v.d, name:v.name, hours:v.hours, est:v.est, calls:v.calls, connects:v.connects, appos:v.appos});
  }
  out.sort(function(a,b){ return a.d===b.d ? (a.name<b.name?-1:1) : (a.d<b.d?-1:1); });
  var apl=[];
  for (var q=1;q<apo.length;q++){
    var md=kanriDateKey_(apo[q][3]); if(!md||md<fromKey) continue;
    apl.push({md:md, mt:kanriHM_(apo[q][4]), project:String(apo[q][2]||""), company:String(apo[q][5]||""), reporter:String(apo[q][1]||"")});
  }
  apl.sort(function(a,b){ return a.md<b.md?-1:(a.md>b.md?1:0); });
  return {days:out, appos:apl};
}


// ============================================================
// v67: 品質ゲート・鍵管理・レート制限・日次スナップショット・自己テスト
// ============================================================
function kanriKey_(){
  try{ var p=PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("KANRI_KEY"); if(p) return String(p); }catch(_e){}
  return KANRI_KEY; // フォールバック（本番はスクリプトプロパティ KANRI_KEY を設定して鍵をコード外へ）
}
function kanriRateOk_(){
  try{
    var c=CacheService.getScriptCache(); var k="kanri_rl_"+Math.floor(Date.now()/60000);
    var n=Number(c.get(k)||0)+1; c.put(k,String(n),90); return n<=6; // 6回/分まで
  }catch(_e){ return true; }
}
// データ品質ゲート：①勤怠の打刻漏れ ②名簿外の名前 ③アポ件数の突合（架電結果の「アポ」 vs アポイント報告）
function kanriQuality_(att, hist, apo, fromKey){
  var known={}; for (var p in FULL_NAME_MAP) known[FULL_NAME_MAP[p]]=1;
  var punchMiss=[], unknown={}, hA={}, rA={};
  for (var i=1;i<att.length;i++){
    var nm=String(att[i][0]||"").trim(); var dk=kanriDateKey_(att[i][1]);
    if(!nm||!dk||dk<fromKey) continue;
    if(!known[nm]) unknown[nm]=(unknown[nm]||0)+1;
    var ci=kanriHM_(att[i][2]), co=kanriHM_(att[i][3]);
    if(ci && !co) punchMiss.push({name:nm, d:dk, note:"出勤あり・退勤なし"});
  }
  for (var j=1;j<hist.length;j++){
    var hn=String(hist[j][0]||"").trim(); var hd=kanriDateKey_(hist[j][1]);
    if(!hn||!hd||hd<fromKey) continue;
    if(!known[hn]) unknown[hn]=(unknown[hn]||0)+1;
    if(mapResultLabelToId(hist[j][6])==="appo"){ var k1=hn+"|"+hd; hA[k1]=(hA[k1]||0)+1; }
  }
  for (var q=1;q<apo.length;q++){
    var rn=String(apo[q][1]||"").trim(); var rd=kanriDateKey_(apo[q][0]);
    if(!rn||!rd||rd<fromKey) continue;
    if(!known[rn]) unknown[rn]=(unknown[rn]||0)+1;
    var k2=rn+"|"+rd; rA[k2]=(rA[k2]||0)+1;
  }
  var mism=[]; var seen={};
  function chk(k){ if(seen[k])return; seen[k]=1;
    var a=hA[k]||0, b=rA[k]||0;
    if(a!==b){ var pr=k.split("|"); mism.push({name:pr[0], d:pr[1], histAppo:a, reported:b, diff:a-b}); } }
  for (var k in hA) chk(k); for (var k2b in rA) chk(k2b);
  var unk=[]; for (var u in unknown) unk.push({name:u, rows:unknown[u]});
  mism.sort(function(x,y){return x.d<y.d?-1:1;});
  return {punchMiss:punchMiss, unknownNames:unk, apoMismatch:mism,
          summary:{punchMiss:punchMiss.length, unknownNames:unk.length, apoMismatch:mism.length}};
}
// 日次スナップショット（時間主導トリガー用）：直近7日の集計を「経営連携_日次集計」へupsert
function kanriSnapshotDaily(){
  var klock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { klock.tryLock(10000); } catch(_le){}
  var ss=SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  function v(nm){ var sh=ss.getSheetByName(nm); return (sh&&sh.getLastRow()>1)?sh.getDataRange().getValues():[]; }
  var fromKey=kanriDateKey_(new Date(Date.now()-7*86400000));
  var out=kanriAggregate_(v("勤怠"),v("架電結果"),v("アポイント"),fromKey);
  var sh=ss.getSheetByName("経営連携_日次集計");
  if(!sh){ sh=ss.insertSheet("経営連携_日次集計"); sh.appendRow(["日付","名前","実働h","推定","架電","接続","アポ","更新時刻"]); }
  var data=sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var idx={};
  for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) idx[kanriDateKey_(data[i][0])+"|"+data[i][1]]=i+1;
  var now=new Date();
  out.days.forEach(function(dv){
    var row=[dv.d,dv.name,dv.hours,dv.est?"推定":"",dv.calls,dv.connects,dv.appos,now];
    var r=idx[dv.d+"|"+dv.name];
    if(r) sh.getRange(r,1,1,8).setValues([row]); else sh.appendRow(row);
  });
  logAudit("kanri_snapshot","trigger","","rows:"+out.days.length);
  try{ kanriEnsureDashboard_(); }catch(e2){ kanriNotifyFail_("dashboard", e2); }
  try{ klock.releaseLock(); }catch(_le3){}
}
function setupKanriTriggers(){ // エディタから1回実行：毎日21時にスナップショット
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t){ if(t.getHandlerFunction()==="kanriSnapshotDaily") ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("kanriSnapshotDaily").timeBased().atHour(21).everyDays(1).create();
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t){ if(t.getHandlerFunction()==="kanriBackupDaily") ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("kanriBackupDaily").timeBased().atHour(22).everyDays(1).create();
}
// 自己テスト（エディタで実行→ログにPASS/FAIL）
function kanriSelfTest_(){
  var att=[["n","d","ci","co","bs","be"],["榮智子","2026/08/17","10:00","18:30","13:00","14:00"],["柏木幸代","2026/08/19","11:00","","",""]];
  var hist=[["n","d","t","c","p","pe","r"]]; 
  for(var i=0;i<3;i++)hist.push(["榮智子","2026/08/17",(10+i)+":10","","","","アポ"]);
  hist.push(["謎の担当","2026/08/17","10:00","","","","不在"]);
  var apo=[["rd","rp","pj","md","mt","co"],["2026/08/17","榮智子","NKT","2026/08/20","10:00","A苑"],["2026/08/17","榮智子","NKT","2026/08/21","10:00","B苑"]];
  var a=kanriAggregate_(att,hist,apo,"2026-08-01");
  var q=kanriQuality_(att,hist,apo,"2026-08-01");
  var t=[["hours7.5",a.days[0].hours===7.5],["appos3",a.days[0].appos===3],
         ["punchMiss1",q.summary.punchMiss===1],["unknown1",q.summary.unknownNames===1],
         ["mismatch(3vs2)",q.summary.apoMismatch===1 && q.apoMismatch[0].diff===1]];
  var ok=t.every(function(x){return x[1];});
  t.forEach(function(x){ Logger.log((x[1]?"PASS ":"FAIL ")+x[0]); });
  Logger.log(ok?"== kanriSelfTest: ALL PASS ==":"== kanriSelfTest: FAILED ==");
  return ok;
}


// ============================================================
// v67: ロール別キー・CSV・双方向プッシュ・準リアルタイムダッシュボード・障害通知
// ============================================================
var KANRI_ADMIN_KEY = "sukima-kanri-admin-2026"; // 管理（push可）。本番はスクリプトプロパティ KANRI_ADMIN_KEY 推奨
var KANRI_ALERT_EMAIL = ""; // 障害通知先（空=通知しない）例: "ichikawa@sukimagroup.jp"

function kanriRole_(key){
  var k=String(key||"");
  if (!k) return "";
  var admin=KANRI_ADMIN_KEY, read=KANRI_KEY;
  try{
    var p=PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    admin=p.getProperty("KANRI_ADMIN_KEY")||admin;
    read=p.getProperty("KANRI_KEY")||read;
  }catch(_e){}
  if (k===admin) return "admin";
  if (k===read) return "read";
  return "";
}
function kanriCsv_(days){
  var out=["date,name,hours,est,calls,connects,appos"];
  (days||[]).forEach(function(d){
    out.push([d.d,'"'+String(d.name).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"',(d.hours==null?"":d.hours),(d.est?1:0),d.calls,d.connects,d.appos].join(","));
  });
  return out.join("\n");
}
function kanriBuildPush_(d){
  if (d.goal!==undefined && d.goal!==null && String(d.goal)!=="") {
    var g=Number(d.goal)||0;
    return {title:"【経営】今月のアポ目標", body:"今月のチーム目標は "+g+" 件です。⑬達成率は経営システムで毎週共有します。", importance:"high"};
  }
  return {title:String(d.title||"【経営】連絡"), body:String(d.message||""), importance:String(d.importance||"high")};
}
// 準リアルタイム・経営ダッシュボード（数式ベース：経営連携_日次集計を参照＝型が正規化済み）
function kanriEnsureDashboard_(){
  var ss=SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sh=ss.getSheetByName("経営ダッシュボード");
  if (!sh) sh=ss.insertSheet("経営ダッシュボード");
  var S="'経営連携_日次集計'";
  function F(day0,colL){ // 期間合計
    return "=IFERROR(SUMPRODUCT(("+S+"!$A$2:$A$10000>=TEXT(TODAY()-"+day0+',"YYYY-MM-DD"))*('+S+"!$A$2:$A$10000<=TEXT(TODAY(),\"YYYY-MM-DD\"))*N("+S+"!$"+colL+"$2:$"+colL+"$10000)),0)";
  }
  var rows=[
    ["経営ダッシュボード（準リアルタイム：5分毎トリガー時）","","","","","","","","今月目標(入力)",60],
    ["指標","本日","直近7日","今月","","担当別・今月アポ","","","今月達成率",'=IFERROR(D4/J1,0)'],
    ["架電",F(0,"E"),F(6,"E"),'=IFERROR(SUMPRODUCT((LEFT('+S+'!$A$2:$A$10000,7)=TEXT(TODAY(),"YYYY-MM"))*N('+S+'!$E$2:$E$10000)),0)',"","名前","アポ","","",""],
    ["アポ",F(0,"G"),F(6,"G"),'=IFERROR(SUMPRODUCT((LEFT('+S+'!$A$2:$A$10000,7)=TEXT(TODAY(),"YYYY-MM"))*N('+S+'!$G$2:$G$10000)),0)',"","","","","",""],
    ["接続",F(0,"F"),F(6,"F"),'=IFERROR(SUMPRODUCT((LEFT('+S+'!$A$2:$A$10000,7)=TEXT(TODAY(),"YYYY-MM"))*N('+S+'!$F$2:$F$10000)),0)',"","","","","",""]
  ];
  sh.getRange(1,1,rows.length,10).setValues(rows.map(function(r){while(r.length<10)r.push("");return r;}));
  var names=[]; for (var p in FULL_NAME_MAP) names.push(FULL_NAME_MAP[p]);
  names=names.filter(function(n){return ["柏木幸代","藤田直美","小澤幸枝","竹之内優子","榮智子","本郷真弓"].indexOf(n)>=0;});
  for (var i=0;i<names.length;i++){
    sh.getRange(4+i,6).setValue(names[i]);
    sh.getRange(4+i,7).setFormula('=IFERROR(SUMPRODUCT((LEFT('+S+'!$A$2:$A$10000,7)=TEXT(TODAY(),"YYYY-MM"))*('+S+'!$B$2:$B$10000="'+names[i]+'")*N('+S+'!$G$2:$G$10000)),0)');
  }
  sh.getRange(2,10).setFormula('=IFERROR($D$4/$J$1,0)');
  // v67: 率KPIと着地予測（営業日=月〜木, weekend="0000111"）
  sh.getRange(6,1).setValue("接続率");
  sh.getRange(6,2).setFormula('=IFERROR(B5/B3,0)'); sh.getRange(6,3).setFormula('=IFERROR(C5/C3,0)'); sh.getRange(6,4).setFormula('=IFERROR(D5/D3,0)');
  sh.getRange(7,1).setValue("アポ率");
  sh.getRange(7,2).setFormula('=IFERROR(B4/B3,0)'); sh.getRange(7,3).setFormula('=IFERROR(C4/C3,0)'); sh.getRange(7,4).setFormula('=IFERROR(D4/D3,0)');
  sh.getRange(3,9).setValue("今月着地予測(アポ)");
  sh.getRange(3,10).setFormula('=IFERROR(ROUND($D$4/MAX(1,NETWORKDAYS.INTL(EOMONTH(TODAY(),-1)+1,TODAY(),"0000111"))*NETWORKDAYS.INTL(EOMONTH(TODAY(),-1)+1,EOMONTH(TODAY(),0),"0000111"),1),0)');
  sh.getRange(4,9).setValue("予測vs目標");
  sh.getRange(4,10).setFormula('=IF($J$3>=$J$1,"✓ 到達ペース","⚠ 不足ペース")');
}
function setupKanriRealtime(){ // エディタから1回実行：5分毎スナップショット＝準リアルタイム
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t){ if(t.getHandlerFunction()==="kanriSnapshotDaily") ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("kanriSnapshotDaily").timeBased().everyMinutes(5).create();
  kanriEnsureDashboard_();
}
function kanriNotifyFail_(where, err){
  try{
    if (!KANRI_ALERT_EMAIL) return;
    MailApp.sendEmail(KANRI_ALERT_EMAIL, "【経営連携】処理失敗: "+where, String(err));
  }catch(_e){}
}


// ============================================================
// v67: 統計的異常検知・労務整合・稼働空転・修復提案・バックアップ/DR
// ============================================================
function kanriQualityV2_(kq, att, hist, shift){
  var today = kanriDateKey_(new Date());
  var mkey = today.slice(0,7);
  // (d) 稼働空転: 本日出勤打刻あり×架電0
  var attToday={}, callToday={};
  for (var i=1;i<att.length;i++){
    var nm=String(att[i][0]||"").trim();
    if (kanriDateKey_(att[i][1])===today && kanriHM_(att[i][2])) attToday[nm]=1;
  }
  for (var j=1;j<hist.length;j++){
    var hn=String(hist[j][0]||"").trim();
    if (kanriDateKey_(hist[j][1])===today) callToday[hn]=(callToday[hn]||0)+1;
  }
  kq.idleAttendance=[];
  for (var nm2 in attToday){ if(!callToday[nm2]) kq.idleAttendance.push({name:nm2, d:today, note:"出勤打刻あり・架電0件"}); }
  // (e) シフト整合: 対象月の最新申請(出勤予定日数) vs 実出勤日数（経過按分・乖離2日以上）
  var latest={};
  for (var s=1;s<shift.length;s++){
    if (String(shift[s][4]||"")!==mkey) continue;
    var ap=String(shift[s][2]||"").trim(); if(!ap) continue;
    latest[ap]=Number(shift[s][5])||0; // 後勝ち=最新
  }
  var attDays={};
  for (var a2=1;a2<att.length;a2++){
    var an=String(att[a2][0]||"").trim(); var ad=kanriDateKey_(att[a2][1]);
    if (ad.slice(0,7)===mkey && kanriHM_(att[a2][2])) { attDays[an]=attDays[an]||{}; attDays[an][ad]=1; }
  }
  var dom = Number(today.slice(8,10)), dim = new Date(Number(today.slice(0,4)), Number(today.slice(5,7)), 0).getDate();
  kq.shiftGap=[];
  for (var p2 in latest){
    var planned=latest[p2]; if(!planned) continue;
    var expect=Math.floor(planned*dom/dim);
    var actual=Object.keys(attDays[p2]||{}).length;
    if (expect-actual>=2) kq.shiftGap.push({name:p2, planned:planned, expected:expect, actual:actual, note:"シフト予定ペース比 "+(expect-actual)+"日不足"});
  }
  // (f) 統計的異常検知: 直近7日アポ率が過去28日基準の50%未満（分母: 両期間とも架電30件以上）
  var d7 = kanriDateKey_(new Date(Date.now()-7*86400000));
  var d35 = kanriDateKey_(new Date(Date.now()-35*86400000));
  var rate={};
  for (var h2=1;h2<hist.length;h2++){
    var rn=String(hist[h2][0]||"").trim(); var rd=kanriDateKey_(hist[h2][1]);
    if (!rn||!rd||rd<d35||rd>today) continue;
    var bucket = (rd>d7)? "recent":"base";
    rate[rn]=rate[rn]||{recent:{c:0,a:0},base:{c:0,a:0}};
    rate[rn][bucket].c++;
    if (mapResultLabelToId(hist[h2][6])==="appo") rate[rn][bucket].a++;
  }
  kq.rateAlerts=[];
  for (var rn2 in rate){
    var R=rate[rn2];
    if (R.recent.c>=30 && R.base.c>=30){
      var pr=R.recent.a/R.recent.c, pb=R.base.a/R.base.c;
      if (pb>0 && pr < pb*0.5) kq.rateAlerts.push({name:rn2, recentRate:Math.round(pr*1000)/10, baseRate:Math.round(pb*1000)/10, note:"直近7日のアポ率が基準期間の半分未満"});
    }
  }
  // 修復提案（突合差異に具体アクションを付与）
  (kq.apoMismatch||[]).forEach(function(m){
    m.fix = (m.diff>0)
      ? m.name+"さんの"+m.d+"のアポ報告が"+m.diff+"件不足→PWAのアポ報告入力を依頼"
      : m.name+"さんの"+m.d+"の架電結果「アポ」が"+(-m.diff)+"件不足→架電結果の記録漏れを確認";
  });
  kq.summary.idleAttendance=kq.idleAttendance.length;
  kq.summary.shiftGap=kq.shiftGap.length;
  kq.summary.rateAlerts=kq.rateAlerts.length;
  return kq;
}
// 日次バックアップ（7世代管理）
var KANRI_BACKUP_KEEP = 7;
function kanriBackupPrune_(names){ // 純関数: "バックアップ_YYYY-MM-DD" 群から削除対象を返す
  var bs = names.filter(function(n){ return /^バックアップ_\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(n); }).sort();
  return bs.slice(0, Math.max(0, bs.length - KANRI_BACKUP_KEEP));
}
function kanriBackupDaily(){
  try{
    var f = DriveApp.getFileById(SHEET_ID);
    var today = kanriDateKey_(new Date());
    var name = "バックアップ_" + today;
    var parent = f.getParents().hasNext() ? f.getParents().next() : DriveApp.getRootFolder();
    var it = parent.getFilesByName(name);
    if (!it.hasNext()) f.makeCopy(name, parent);
    var all=[], files=parent.getFiles();
    while (files.hasNext()){ var g=files.next(); all.push(g.getName()); }
    kanriBackupPrune_(all).forEach(function(del){
      var di=parent.getFilesByName(del);
      while (di.hasNext()) di.next().setTrashed(true);
    });
    logAudit("kanri_backup","trigger","",name);
  }catch(e){ kanriNotifyFail_("backup", e); }
}


// ══════════ v70: 通話録音の自動収集（v69機能を統合） ══════════
var REC_CONFIG_SHEET = "録音設定";
var REC_CONFIG_HEADER = ["PIN", "名前", "ドライブフォルダID", "照合許容(分)", "有効", "最終スキャン", "前回結果"];
var REC_EXT = ["m4a", "mp3", "amr", "3gp", "aac", "wav", "ogg", "opus"];
var REC_ALERT_PCT = 70;   // これ未満のカバー率で通知する
var REC_SCAN_FN = "autoRecScan";
var REC_DONE_LABEL = "録音取込済";
var REC_INBOX_SHEET = "録音取込ログ";
var REC_MAX_MB = 25;   // 1ファイルの上限（Gmail添付の実用上限）

function recDigits_(s) { return String(s || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); }

function recParseStamp_(fileName, fallbackMs) {
  var t = String(fileName || "");
  var m = t.match(/(20\d{2})[-_\/.]?(\d{2})[-_\/.]?(\d{2})[ _T-]+(\d{2})[-_:;.]?(\d{2})(?:[-_:;.]?(\d{2}))?/);
  if (m) {
    return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]),
      Number(m[4]), Number(m[5]), Number(m[6] || 0)).getTime();
  }
  m = t.match(/(20\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})/);
  if (m) {
    return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]),
      Number(m[4]), Number(m[5]), Number(m[6])).getTime();
  }
  return Number(fallbackMs) || 0;
}

function recPhoneIn_(fileName) {
  var d = String(fileName || "").replace(/20\d{6}[\d_\-:;.]*/g, " ");  // 日時部分を除外
  var m = d.match(/\d[\d\-()\s]{7,}\d/g);
  if (!m) return "";
  for (var i = 0; i < m.length; i++) {
    var v = recDigits_(m[i]);
    if (v.length >= 9 && v.length <= 11) return v;
  }
  return "";
}

function recIsAudio_(name) {
  var e = String(name || "").split(".").pop().toLowerCase();
  return REC_EXT.indexOf(e) >= 0;
}

function recMatchFiles_(calls, files, tolMin) {
  var tol = (Number(tolMin) || 5) * 60000;
  var wide = tol * 18;   // 長電話で録音の時刻が終了時刻になる場合に備えた広い窓（既定90分）
  var used = {}, taken = {}, assign = [];
  var byPhone = {};
  calls.forEach(function (c, i) {
    var p = recDigits_(c.phone);
    if (p) { (byPhone[p] = byPhone[p] || []).push(i); }
  });
  function phoneOf(f) { return f.phone || recPhoneIn_(f.name); }
  // 指定した条件で、まだ空いている架電のうち最も時刻が近いものを選ぶ
  function pick(f, idxList, limit) {
    var best = -1, bestDiff = Infinity;
    (idxList || []).forEach(function (i) {
      if (used[i]) return;
      var d = Math.abs(calls[i].atMs - f.atMs);
      if (d <= limit && d < bestDiff) { best = i; bestDiff = d; }
    });
    return { i: best, diff: bestDiff };
  }
  function allIdx() { var a = []; for (var i = 0; i < calls.length; i++) a.push(i); return a; }
  function commit(f, r) {
    used[r.i] = true; taken[f.id] = true;
    assign.push({ call: calls[r.i], file: f, diffMs: r.diff });
  }
  // ① 電話番号が一致し、時刻も近いもの（もっとも確実）
  files.forEach(function (f) {
    var p = phoneOf(f);
    if (!p || !byPhone[p]) return;
    var r = pick(f, byPhone[p], tol);
    if (r.i >= 0) commit(f, r);
  });
  // ② 電話番号は分からないが、時刻がごく近いもの
  files.forEach(function (f) {
    if (taken[f.id]) return;
    if (phoneOf(f) && byPhone[phoneOf(f)]) return;   // 番号ありは③で扱う
    var r = pick(f, allIdx(), tol);
    if (r.i >= 0) commit(f, r);
  });
  // ③ 電話番号は一致するが時刻が離れているもの（長電話などを救済）
  files.forEach(function (f) {
    if (taken[f.id]) return;
    var p = phoneOf(f);
    if (!p || !byPhone[p]) return;
    var r = pick(f, byPhone[p], wide);
    if (r.i >= 0) commit(f, r);
  });
  var unmatched = files.filter(function (f) { return !taken[f.id]; });
  var noRec = [];
  calls.forEach(function (c, i) { if (!used[i]) noRec.push(c); });
  return { assign: assign, unmatchedFiles: unmatched, callsWithoutRec: noRec };
}

function extractFolderId_(v) {
  var t = String(v || "").trim();
  var m = t.match(/\/folders\/([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)/);
  if (m) return m[1];
  m = t.match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)/);
  return m ? m[1] : t;
}

function recFolderName_(pin) {
  var nm = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(pin || "").trim()];
  if (!nm) return "";
  return nm + "_" + String(pin).trim();     // 例: 竹之内優子_098
}

function recDatePath_(atMs) {
  var d = new Date(Number(atMs) || 0);
  if (!atMs || isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  var y = d.getFullYear(), m = ("0" + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2), dd = ("0" + d.getDate()).slice(-2);
  return { ym: y + "-" + m, d: dd, key: y + "-" + m + "-" + dd };
}

function recChildFolder_(parent, name) {
  var it = parent.getFoldersByName(name);
  if (it.hasNext()) return it.next();
  return parent.createFolder(name);
}

function provisionRecFolders(rootIdOrUrl) {
  var rootId = extractFolderId_(rootIdOrUrl);
  var root;
  try { root = rootId ? DriveApp.getFolderById(rootId) : DriveApp.createFolder("通話録音"); }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, message: "親フォルダを開けません: " + e }; }

  var out = [];
  Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (pin) {
    var fname = recFolderName_(pin);
    if (!fname) return;
    var folder;
    try { folder = recChildFolder_(root, fname); }
    catch (e2) { out.push({ pin: pin, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[pin], ok: false, message: String(e2) }); return; }
    // 本人のGmailに編集権限で共有（本人の端末からアップロードできるようにするため）
    var mail = personalEmailFor(pin), shared = "";
    if (mail) {
      try { folder.addEditor(mail); shared = mail; }
      catch (e3) { shared = "共有失敗(" + mail + ")"; }
    }
    // 「録音設定」に自動登録（管理者が手でURLを貼る必要をなくす）
    try {
      writeRecConfig_({ pin: pin, folderId: folder.getId(), tol: 5, enabled: true });
    } catch (e4) {}
    out.push({ pin: pin, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[pin], ok: true,
      folderId: folder.getId(), url: folder.getUrl(), shared: shared });
  });
  // v69: 「受信箱」フォルダも用意する（誰でもここに置けば自動で振り分けられる）
  var inbox = null;
  try {
    inbox = recChildFolder_(root, "_受信箱");
    Object.keys(STAFF_EMAILS).forEach(function (pn) {
      var em = STAFF_EMAILS[pn];
      if (em) { try { inbox.addEditor(em); } catch (e) {} }
    });
  } catch (e2) {}
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    props.setProperty("REC_ROOT_FOLDER", root.getId());
    if (inbox) props.setProperty("REC_INBOX_FOLDER", inbox.getId());
  } catch (e3) {}
  logAudit("録音フォルダ整備", "管理者", out.length + "名分を作成/共有", "親:" + root.getName());
  return { ok: true, rootId: root.getId(), rootUrl: root.getUrl(), rootName: root.getName(),
    inboxId: inbox ? inbox.getId() : "", inboxUrl: inbox ? inbox.getUrl() : "", members: out };
}

function recOrganizeFolder_(folder, sinceMs) {
  var moved = 0, kept = 0;
  var it = folder.getFiles(), guard = 0;
  var buckets = {};
  while (it.hasNext() && guard < 500) {
    guard++;
    var f = it.next();
    var nm = f.getName();
    if (!recIsAudio_(nm)) continue;
    var atMs = recParseStamp_(nm, f.getDateCreated().getTime());
    if (sinceMs && atMs < sinceMs) { kept++; continue; }
    var pathInfo = recDatePath_(atMs);
    if (!pathInfo) { kept++; continue; }
    var key = pathInfo.ym + "/" + pathInfo.d;
    if (!buckets[key]) {
      var ymF = recChildFolder_(folder, pathInfo.ym);
      buckets[key] = recChildFolder_(ymF, pathInfo.d);
    }
    try { buckets[key].addFile(f); folder.removeFile(f); moved++; }
    catch (e) { kept++; }
  }
  return { moved: moved, kept: kept };
}

function recSyncVerdict_(lastUploadMs, todayCalls, todayFiles, nowMs) {
  if (!lastUploadMs) return todayCalls > 0 ? "未着信" : "未設定";
  var hours = (nowMs - lastUploadMs) / 3600000;
  if (todayCalls > 0 && todayFiles === 0) return "停止の疑い";
  if (hours > 72) return "3日以上なし";
  if (hours > 24) return "24時間以上なし";
  return "正常";
}

function recPinFromEmail_(from) {
  var addr = String(from || "").toLowerCase();
  var m = addr.match(/[a-z0-9._%+-]+@[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/);
  if (m) addr = m[0];
  for (var pin in STAFF_EMAILS) {
    var e = String(STAFF_EMAILS[pin] || "").toLowerCase();
    if (e && e === addr) return String(pin);
  }
  return "";
}

function recPinFromText_(text) {
  var t = String(text || "");
  for (var pin in FULL_NAME_MAP) {
    var nm = FULL_NAME_MAP[pin];
    if (nm && t.indexOf(nm) >= 0) return String(pin);
    var sh = PIN_MAP[pin];
    if (sh && t.indexOf(sh) >= 0) return String(pin);
  }
  var m = t.match(/(?:^|[^0-9])(0?9[1-8]|10[135]|108)(?:[^0-9]|$)/);
  if (m) {
    var cand = m[1].length === 2 ? "0" + m[1] : m[1];
    if (FULL_NAME_MAP[cand]) return cand;
  }
  return "";
}

function recIngestDecide_(msg) {
  var pin = recPinFromEmail_(msg.from);
  if (!pin) pin = recPinFromText_(msg.subject || "");
  if (!pin) pin = recPinFromText_(msg.fileName || "");
  return pin;
}

function recAttachmentOk_(name, sizeBytes) {
  if (!recIsAudio_(name)) return false;
  if (sizeBytes && sizeBytes > REC_MAX_MB * 1024 * 1024) return false;
  return true;
}

function recDupKey_(pin, name, sizeBytes) {
  return String(pin) + "|" + String(name || "") + "|" + String(sizeBytes || 0);
}

function recLoadIngested_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sh = ensureSheet(ss, REC_INBOX_SHEET,
    ["取込日時", "経路", "PIN", "名前", "ファイル名", "サイズ", "保存先", "重複鍵"]);
  var seen = {};
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var v = sh.getRange(2, 8, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { if (v[i][0]) seen[String(v[i][0])] = true; }
  }
  return { sheet: sh, seen: seen };
}

function recMemberFolder_(pin, rootFolder) {
  // v84: ご指定のフォルダがあれば最優先で使う
  var fixed = REC_MEMBER_FOLDERS[String(pin)];
  if (fixed) {
    try { return DriveApp.getFolderById(fixed); }
    catch (eF) { Logger.log("fixed folder open failed: " + pin + " " + eF); }
  }
  var cfgs = readRecConfigs_();
  for (var i = 0; i < cfgs.length; i++) {
    if (String(cfgs[i].pin) === String(pin) && cfgs[i].folderId) {
      try { return DriveApp.getFolderById(cfgs[i].folderId); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  if (!rootFolder) return null;
  var nm = recFolderName_(pin);
  if (!nm) return null;
  var f = recChildFolder_(rootFolder, nm);
  try { writeRecConfig_({ pin: pin, folderId: f.getId(), tol: 5, enabled: true }); } catch (e2) {}
  return f;
}

function recSaveBlob_(memberFolder, blob, atMs) {
  var dp = recDatePath_(atMs) || recDatePath_(new Date().getTime());
  var ymF = recChildFolder_(memberFolder, dp.ym);
  var dF = recChildFolder_(ymF, dp.d);
  var file = dF.createFile(blob);
  return file;
}

function recIngestGmail_(days, rootFolderId) {
  var rep = { channel: "メール", saved: 0, skipped: 0, unknown: 0, details: [] };
  var root = null;
  if (rootFolderId) { try { root = DriveApp.getFolderById(extractFolderId_(rootFolderId)); } catch (e) {} }
  var label;
  try { label = GmailApp.getUserLabelByName(REC_DONE_LABEL) || GmailApp.createLabel(REC_DONE_LABEL); }
  catch (e2) { rep.error = "ラベルを用意できません: " + e2; return rep; }

  var q = 'has:attachment newer_than:' + (Number(days) || 7) + 'd -label:"' + REC_DONE_LABEL + '"';
  var threads = GmailApp.search(q, 0, 40);
  var log = recLoadIngested_();
  var rows = [];
  for (var t = 0; t < threads.length; t++) {
    var msgs = threads[t].getMessages();
    var touched = false;
    for (var m = 0; m < msgs.length; m++) {
      var msg = msgs[m];
      var atts = msg.getAttachments();
      for (var a = 0; a < atts.length; a++) {
        var att = atts[a];
        var nm = att.getName(), sz = att.getSize();
        if (!recAttachmentOk_(nm, sz)) { continue; }
        var pin = recIngestDecide_({ from: msg.getFrom(), subject: msg.getSubject(), fileName: nm });
        if (!pin) { rep.unknown++; rep.details.push("差出人不明: " + nm); continue; }
        var key = recDupKey_(pin, nm, sz);
        if (log.seen[key]) { rep.skipped++; touched = true; continue; }
        var folder = recMemberFolder_(pin, root);
        if (!folder) { rep.details.push("保存先なし: " + (FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || pin)); continue; }
        var atMs = recParseStamp_(nm, msg.getDate().getTime());
        try {
          var f = recSaveBlob_(folder, att.copyBlob().setName(nm), atMs);
          var au = "";
          try { au = recArchiveCopy_(f, pin, atMs, nm); } catch (eA3) {}
          rows.push([new Date(), "メール", pin, FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || "", nm, sz,
            f.getUrl() + (au ? " / 保管庫:" + au : ""), key]);
          log.seen[key] = true;
          rep.saved++; touched = true;
        } catch (e3) { rep.details.push("保存失敗: " + nm + " " + e3); }
      }
    }
    if (touched) { try { threads[t].addLabel(label); } catch (e4) {} }
  }
  if (rows.length) { log.sheet.getRange(log.sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, 8).setValues(rows); }
  return rep;
}

function recIngestInbox_(inboxFolderId, rootFolderId) {
  var rep = { channel: "受信箱", saved: 0, skipped: 0, unknown: 0, details: [] };
  if (!inboxFolderId) { rep.details.push("受信箱フォルダが未設定"); return rep; }
  var inbox, root = null;
  try { inbox = DriveApp.getFolderById(extractFolderId_(inboxFolderId)); }
  catch (e) { rep.error = "受信箱を開けません: " + e; return rep; }
  if (rootFolderId) { try { root = DriveApp.getFolderById(extractFolderId_(rootFolderId)); } catch (e2) {} }

  var log = recLoadIngested_();
  var rows = [], it = inbox.getFiles(), guard = 0;
  while (it.hasNext() && guard < 300) {
    guard++;
    var f = it.next();
    var nm = f.getName(), sz = f.getSize();
    if (!recAttachmentOk_(nm, sz)) continue;
    var pin = recPinFromText_(nm);
    if (!pin) { try { pin = recPinFromEmail_(f.getOwner() ? f.getOwner().getEmail() : ""); } catch (e3) {} }
    if (!pin) { rep.unknown++; rep.details.push("担当者不明: " + nm); continue; }
    var key = recDupKey_(pin, nm, sz);
    if (log.seen[key]) { rep.skipped++; continue; }
    var folder = recMemberFolder_(pin, root);
    if (!folder) { rep.details.push("保存先なし: " + (FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || pin)); continue; }
    var atMs = recParseStamp_(nm, f.getDateCreated().getTime());
    try {
      var saved = recSaveBlob_(folder, f.getBlob().setName(nm), atMs);
      inbox.removeFile(f);   // 受信箱から片付ける（担当者フォルダへ移動した形にする）
      var au2 = "";
      try { au2 = recArchiveCopy_(saved, pin, atMs, nm); } catch (eA4) {}
      rows.push([new Date(), "受信箱", pin, FULL_NAME_MAP[pin] || "", nm, sz,
        saved.getUrl() + (au2 ? " / 保管庫:" + au2 : ""), key]);
      log.seen[key] = true;
      rep.saved++;
    } catch (e4) { rep.details.push("保存失敗: " + nm + " " + e4); }
  }
  if (rows.length) { log.sheet.getRange(log.sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, 8).setValues(rows); }
  return rep;
}

function recIngestAll(days, rootFolderId, inboxFolderId) {
  var out = [];
  try { out.push(recIngestGmail_(days, rootFolderId)); } catch (e) { out.push({ channel: "メール", error: String(e) }); }
  if (inboxFolderId) {
    try { out.push(recIngestInbox_(inboxFolderId, rootFolderId)); } catch (e2) { out.push({ channel: "受信箱", error: String(e2) }); }
  }
  var saved = 0; out.forEach(function (r) { saved += (r.saved || 0); });
  logAudit("録音吸い上げ", "システム", saved + "件を保存", out.map(function (r) { return r.channel + ":" + (r.saved || 0); }).join(" / "));
  return out;
}

function readRecConfigs_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sh = ensureSheet(ss, REC_CONFIG_SHEET, REC_CONFIG_HEADER);
  var out = [];
  if (sh.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  var rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    if (!String(rows[i][0] || "").trim()) continue;
    out.push({
      row: i + 1, pin: String(rows[i][0]).trim(), name: String(rows[i][1] || ""),
      folderId: extractFolderId_(rows[i][2]), tol: Number(rows[i][3]) || 5,
      enabled: !(String(rows[i][4]) === "false" || rows[i][4] === false),
      lastScan: rows[i][5] ? formatDateTimeStr(rows[i][5]) : "",
      lastResult: String(rows[i][6] || "")
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function writeRecConfig_(c) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sh = ensureSheet(ss, REC_CONFIG_SHEET, REC_CONFIG_HEADER);
  var row = [String(c.pin), FULL_NAME_MAP[c.pin] || "", extractFolderId_(c.folderId),
    Number(c.tol) || 5, c.enabled === false ? "false" : "true", "", ""];
  var found = -1;
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (String(v[i][0]).trim() === String(c.pin).trim()) { found = i + 1; break; } }
  }
  if (found > 0) {
    var keep = sh.getRange(found, 6, 1, 2).getValues()[0];
    row[5] = keep[0]; row[6] = keep[1];
    sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
  } else { sh.appendRow(row); }
  logAudit("録音設定", "管理者", (FULL_NAME_MAP[c.pin] || c.pin), "フォルダ:" + row[2]);
}

function recScanFor_(cfg, days) {
  var rep = { pin: cfg.pin, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[cfg.pin] || cfg.name, ok: false,
    files: 0, matched: 0, unmatched: 0, noRec: 0, message: "" };
  if (!cfg.enabled) { rep.message = "無効"; return rep; }
  if (!cfg.folderId) { rep.message = "ドライブフォルダが未設定です"; return rep; }
  var folder;
  try { folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(cfg.folderId); }
  catch (e) { rep.message = "フォルダを開けません（共有設定をご確認ください）"; return rep; }

  var since = new Date().getTime() - (Number(days) || 7) * 86400000;
  // v68: 直下に上がってきた録音を「年月/日」へ自動整理してから読み取る
  try { var org = recOrganizeFolder_(folder, 0); rep.organized = org.moved; } catch (eo) { rep.organized = 0; }
  // 直下＋小フォルダ（2階層）をまとめて走査する
  var files = [], guard = 0, lastUpload = 0;
  function scanFolder(fd, depth) {
    var it = fd.getFiles();
    while (it.hasNext() && guard < 1200) {
      guard++;
      var f = it.next();
      var nm = f.getName();
      if (!recIsAudio_(nm)) continue;
      var created = f.getDateCreated().getTime();
      if (created > lastUpload) lastUpload = created;
      var atMs = recParseStamp_(nm, created);
      if (atMs < since) continue;
      files.push({ id: f.getId(), name: nm, atMs: atMs, phone: recPhoneIn_(nm), url: f.getUrl() });
    }
    if (depth <= 0) return;
    var sub = fd.getFolders();
    while (sub.hasNext() && guard < 1200) { scanFolder(sub.next(), depth - 1); }
  }
  scanFolder(folder, 2);
  rep.files = files.length;
  rep.lastUpload = lastUpload ? formatDateTimeStr(new Date(lastUpload)) : "";
  rep.lastUploadMs = lastUpload;

  // 架電結果から対象者の分を取り出す
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var crSh = ss.getSheetByName("架電結果");
  if (!crSh || crSh.getLastRow() < 2) { rep.message = "架電結果がありません"; return rep; }
  var crRows = crSh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var myName = FULL_NAME_MAP[cfg.pin] || cfg.name;
  var calls = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < crRows.length; i++) {
    if (String(crRows[i][0] || "").trim() !== String(myName).trim()) continue;
    var ymd = normalizeYMD(crRows[i][1]);
    var hm = hhmm_(crRows[i][2]);
    if (!ymd || !hm) continue;
    var atMs2 = new Date(Number(ymd.slice(0, 4)), Number(ymd.slice(5, 7)) - 1, Number(ymd.slice(8, 10)),
      Number(hm.slice(0, 2)), Number(hm.slice(3, 5)), 0).getTime();
    if (atMs2 < since) continue;
    calls.push({ row: i + 1, phone: String(crRows[i][4] || ""), atMs: atMs2,
      company: String(crRows[i][3] || ""), date: ymd, time: hm, name: myName,
      hasRec: !!String(crRows[i][16] || "") });
  }
  calls.sort(function (a, b) { return a.atMs - b.atMs; });

  // v86: 担当者フォルダに入っている録音を保管庫へも写す（同期アプリ経由の分を拾う）
  try { var sw = recArchiveSweep_(cfg.pin, days || 7); rep.archived = sw.copied; }
  catch (eSw) { rep.archived = 0; }

  var res = recMatchFiles_(calls, files, cfg.tol);
  // 書き戻し（録音ファイル=17列目 / 録音URL=18列目）
  res.assign.forEach(function (a) {
    try { crSh.getRange(a.call.row, 17, 1, 2).setValues([[a.file.name, a.file.url]]); } catch (e) {}
  });
  rep.ok = true;
  rep.matched = res.assign.length;
  rep.unmatched = res.unmatchedFiles.length;
  rep.noRec = res.callsWithoutRec.length;
  rep.noRecList = res.callsWithoutRec.slice(0, 8).map(function (c) { return c.company + "(" + c.time + ")"; });
  rep.message = "録音 " + rep.files + "件中 " + rep.matched + "件を紐づけ／未紐づけ録音 " + rep.unmatched
    + "件／録音なしの架電 " + rep.noRec + "件"
    + (rep.organized ? "／日付フォルダへ整理 " + rep.organized + "件" : "")
    + (rep.archived ? "／保管庫へ " + rep.archived + "件" : "");
  // v68: 同期が止まっていないかの判定を添える
  try {
    var todayKey = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Tokyo", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    var tCalls = 0, tFiles = 0;
    calls.forEach(function (c) { if (c.date === todayKey) tCalls++; });
    files.forEach(function (f) {
      var dp = recDatePath_(f.atMs);
      if (dp && dp.key === todayKey) tFiles++;
    });
    rep.todayCalls = tCalls; rep.todayFiles = tFiles;
    rep.sync = recSyncVerdict_(rep.lastUploadMs, tCalls, tFiles, new Date().getTime());
  } catch (ev) { rep.sync = ""; }
  try {
    var csh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID).getSheetByName(REC_CONFIG_SHEET);
    if (csh && cfg.row) csh.getRange(cfg.row, 6, 1, 2).setValues([[new Date(), rep.message]]);
  } catch (e) {}
  logAudit("録音スキャン", "管理者", rep.name, rep.message);
  return rep;
}

function recScanAll(pinFilter, days) {
  var cfgs = readRecConfigs_(), out = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < cfgs.length; i++) {
    if (pinFilter && String(cfgs[i].pin) !== String(pinFilter)) continue;
    out.push(recScanFor_(cfgs[i], days));
  }
  return out;
}

function autoRecScan() {
  // v69: まず端末から届いた録音を吸い上げてから照合する
  var ingest = [];
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    ingest = recIngestAll(3, props.getProperty("REC_ROOT_FOLDER") || "", props.getProperty("REC_INBOX_FOLDER") || "");
  } catch (eIn) { Logger.log("ingest failed: " + eIn); }
  var reps = recScanAll(null, 3);
  // v65: カバー率が低いメンバーを管理者へ通知（気づきの自動化）
  try {
    var alerts = [];
    reps.forEach(function (r) {
      if (!r.ok) { alerts.push("・" + r.name + "：" + r.message); return; }
      var denom = r.matched + r.noRec;
      if (denom >= 5) {
        var pct = Math.round(r.matched / denom * 100);
        if (pct < REC_ALERT_PCT) {
          alerts.push("・" + r.name + "：録音カバー率 " + pct + "%（" + r.matched + "/" + denom + "件）");
        }
      }
      if (r.unmatched > 0) {
        alerts.push("・" + r.name + "：架電に紐づかない録音が " + r.unmatched + "件");
      }
    });
    if (alerts.length) {
      MailApp.sendEmail(DAILY_REPORT_TO,
        "【録音チェック】確認が必要な項目が " + alerts.length + "件あります",
        "通話録音の自動照合を実行しました。次の点をご確認ください。\n\n" + alerts.join("\n") +
        "\n\n※ カバー率が低い場合は、端末の「通話を自動録音」がONか、ドライブへの同期が動いているかをご確認ください。\n\n" +
        "川嶋メソッド テレアポ業務管理システム");
      logAudit("録音アラート", "システム", alerts.length + "件", "");
    }
  } catch (e) { Logger.log("rec alert failed: " + e); }
}

function getRecTriggerStatus() {
  var trs = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var i = 0; i < trs.length; i++) {
    if (trs[i].getHandlerFunction() === REC_SCAN_FN) return { on: true };
  }
  return { on: false };
}

function setRecTrigger(on, hours) {
  var trs = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var i = 0; i < trs.length; i++) {
    if (trs[i].getHandlerFunction() === REC_SCAN_FN) ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trs[i]);
  }
  if (!on) { logAudit("録音自動照合", "管理者", "停止", ""); return { on: false }; }
  var hh = Number(hours) || 24;
  if (hh >= 24) { ScriptApp.newTrigger(REC_SCAN_FN).timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(21).create(); }
  else { ScriptApp.newTrigger(REC_SCAN_FN).timeBased().everyHours(Math.max(1, hh)).create(); }
  logAudit("録音自動照合", "管理者", "開始（" + (hh >= 24 ? "毎日21時" : hh + "時間ごと") + "）", "");
  return { on: true, hours: hh };
}

function recListFor_(pin, month, resultFilter, limit) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName("架電結果");
  var out = [];
  if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  var nm = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(pin || "")] || "";
  var rows = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = rows.length - 1; i >= 1 && out.length < (limit || 100); i--) {
    var name = String(rows[i][0] || "").trim();
    if (pin && name !== nm) continue;
    var ymd = normalizeYMD(rows[i][1]);
    if (month && (!ymd || ymd.slice(0, 7) !== month)) continue;
    var result = String(rows[i][6] || "");
    if (resultFilter && result !== resultFilter) continue;
    var url = String(rows[i][17] || "");
    if (!url) continue;
    out.push({
      name: name, date: ymd, time: String(rows[i][2] || ""),
      company: String(rows[i][3] || ""), phone: String(rows[i][4] || ""),
      result: result, dur: String(rows[i][7] || ""),
      file: String(rows[i][16] || ""), url: url
    });
  }
  return out;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v70: 架電システム → 経営システム への「即時反映」
//   これまでは5分ごとの再集計（ポーリング）だったため、入力から
//   最大5分の遅れがあった。v70では保存された瞬間に該当する
//   「日付×担当者」の1行だけを更新する（イベント駆動）。
//   ・O(1)の加算なので、何件保存されても重くならない
//   ・5分ごとの全再計算はそのまま残し、ズレがあっても自動で直る
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var KANRI_LIVE_SHEET = "経営連携_日次集計";
var KANRI_LIVE_HEADER = ["日付", "名前", "実働h", "推定", "架電", "接続", "アポ", "更新時刻"];

// 架電結果1件が「接続」「アポ」に当たるかを判定（kanriAggregate_ と同一基準）
function kanriDelta_(resultLabel) {
  var rid = mapResultLabelToId(resultLabel);
  return {
    calls: 1,
    connects: (rid !== "absent" && rid !== "wrong") ? 1 : 0,
    appos: (rid === "appo") ? 1 : 0
  };
}
// 既存行に増分を足した新しい行を作る（純粋関数＝検証しやすい）
function kanriApplyDelta_(row, delta, now) {
  var r = row ? row.slice(0) : ["", "", null, "", 0, 0, 0, ""];
  r[4] = (Number(r[4]) || 0) + (delta.calls || 0);
  r[5] = (Number(r[5]) || 0) + (delta.connects || 0);
  r[6] = (Number(r[6]) || 0) + (delta.appos || 0);
  r[7] = now;
  return r;
}
// 勤怠から実働時間を求める（kanriAggregate_ と同一基準）
function kanriHoursFrom_(ci, co, bs, be) {
  var a = kanriMin_(kanriHM_(ci)), b = kanriMin_(kanriHM_(co));
  if (a === null || b === null || b <= a) return null;
  var s = kanriMin_(kanriHM_(bs)), e = kanriMin_(kanriHM_(be));
  var brk = (s !== null && e !== null && e > s) ? (e - s) : 0;
  return Math.round(((b - a - brk) / 60) * 100) / 100;
}
// 「日付×担当者」の行番号を探す（キャッシュ付き）
function kanriFindRow_(sh, dateKey, name) {
  var ck = "kanri_row_" + dateKey + "_" + name;
  try {
    var c = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var hit = c.get(ck);
    if (hit) {
      var rn = Number(hit);
      var v = sh.getRange(rn, 1, 1, 2).getValues()[0];
      if (kanriDateKey_(v[0]) === dateKey && String(v[1]).trim() === name) return rn;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  if (sh.getLastRow() < 2) return 0;
  var data = sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (kanriDateKey_(data[i][0]) === dateKey && String(data[i][1]).trim() === name) {
      var rowNo = i + 2;
      try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(ck, String(rowNo), 21600); } catch (e2) {}
      return rowNo;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}
// 即時反映の本体。kind: "call"（架電結果）／"appo"（アポ報告）／"hours"（勤怠）
function kanriLiveUpdate_(name, dateKey, kind, payload) {
  if (!name || !dateKey) return false;
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.tryLock(8000); } catch (e) {}
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(KANRI_LIVE_SHEET);
    if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(KANRI_LIVE_SHEET); sh.appendRow(KANRI_LIVE_HEADER); }
    var rowNo = kanriFindRow_(sh, dateKey, name);
    var cur = rowNo ? sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, 8).getValues()[0] : null;
    var now = new Date();
    var next;
    if (kind === "hours") {
      next = cur ? cur.slice(0) : [dateKey, name, null, "", 0, 0, 0, now];
      next[2] = payload.hours;
      next[3] = "";               // 実測が入ったので「推定」を外す
      next[7] = now;
    } else {
      var delta = (kind === "appo") ? { calls: 0, connects: 0, appos: 1 } : kanriDelta_(payload.result);
      next = kanriApplyDelta_(cur, delta, now);
    }
    next[0] = dateKey; next[1] = name;
    if (rowNo) { sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, 8).setValues([next]); }
    else {
      sh.appendRow(next);
      try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put("kanri_row_" + dateKey + "_" + name, String(sh.getLastRow()), 21600); } catch (e2) {}
    }
    // 経営側が「いつの時点のデータか」を判断できるように最終反映時刻を残す
    try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("KANRI_LAST_LIVE", now.toISOString()); } catch (e3) {}
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("kanriLiveUpdate_ failed: " + err);
    return false;
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e4) {}
  }
}
// 架電結果の保存直後に呼ぶ
function kanriOnCallResult_(d) {
  try {
    return kanriLiveUpdate_(String(d.name || "").trim(), kanriDateKey_(d.date), "call", { result: d.result });
  } catch (e) { return false; }
}
// アポ報告の保存直後に呼ぶ（報告日ベース＝品質ゲートの突合と同じ基準）
function kanriOnAppointment_(d) {
  try {
    return kanriLiveUpdate_(String(d.reporter || d.name || "").trim(), kanriDateKey_(d.date), "appo", {});
  } catch (e) { return false; }
}
// 出退勤の保存直後に呼ぶ
function kanriOnAttendance_(d) {
  try {
    var h = kanriHoursFrom_(d.clockIn, d.clockOut, d.breakStart, d.breakEnd);
    if (h === null) return false;
    return kanriLiveUpdate_(String(d.name || "").trim(), kanriDateKey_(d.date), "hours", { hours: h });
  } catch (e) { return false; }
}

function setupRecording() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  ensureSheet(ss, REC_CONFIG_SHEET, REC_CONFIG_HEADER);
  ensureSheet(ss, REC_SRC_SHEET, REC_SRC_HEADER);
  ensureSheet(ss, REC_INBOX_SHEET, ["取込日時","経路","PIN","名前","ファイル名","サイズ","保存先","重複鍵"]);
  var cr = ss.getSheetByName("架電結果");
  if (cr) {
    var lastCol = cr.getLastColumn();
    var hdr = cr.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(1, lastCol)).getValues()[0];
    if (hdr.indexOf("録音ファイル") < 0) {
      cr.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, 2).setValues([["録音ファイル", "録音URL"]]);
      cr.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, 2).setFontWeight("bold");
    }
  }
  Logger.log("✅ 録音管理の初期化が完了しました");
}

function recHandle_(d) {
  function J(o){return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(o)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON)}
  try {
    if (d.type === "kanri_push_diagnose") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: kanriPushDiagnose() });
    }
    if (d.type === "kanri_push_now") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: kanriPushDay_(String(d.name || ""), d.date || kanriDateKey_(new Date())) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_pending") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recPendingFor(d.pin, d.days || 3) });
    }
    if (d.type === "nightly_twice") {
      return J({ status: "ok", trigger: setNightlyTwice(d.on === true, d.hour, d.hour2) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_archive_sweep") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recArchiveSweepAll(d.days || 14) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_long_calls") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recLongCalls(d.days || 7) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_archive_check") {
      var acOut = { folderId: REC_ARCHIVE_FOLDER, ok: false, url: "", months: [], files: 0 };
      try {
        var arcF = DriveApp.getFolderById(REC_ARCHIVE_FOLDER);
        acOut.ok = true; acOut.url = arcF.getUrl(); acOut.name = arcF.getName();
        var it2 = arcF.getFolders(), cnt = 0;
        while (it2.hasNext() && cnt < 6) { acOut.months.push(it2.next().getName()); cnt++; }
        // 直近の保存例を数件
        var samples = [], guard2 = 0;
        var ymIt = arcF.getFolders();
        while (ymIt.hasNext() && samples.length < 5 && guard2 < 30) {
          guard2++;
          var ymF = ymIt.next(), dIt = ymF.getFolders();
          while (dIt.hasNext() && samples.length < 5) {
            var dF = dIt.next(), fIt = dF.getFiles();
            while (fIt.hasNext() && samples.length < 5) {
              var sf = fIt.next();
              samples.push({ name: sf.getName(), path: ymF.getName() + "/" + dF.getName(), url: sf.getUrl() });
            }
          }
        }
        acOut.samples = samples;
        acOut.files = samples.length;
      } catch (eAC) { acOut.message = "開けません（共有をご確認ください）: " + eAC; }
      return J({ status: "ok", archive: acOut });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_folder_check") {
      var fcOut = [];
      Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (pn) {
        var id = REC_MEMBER_FOLDERS[pn] || "";
        var row = { pin: pn, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[pn], folderId: id, ok: false, url: "", message: "" };
        if (!id) { row.message = "フォルダ未設定"; fcOut.push(row); return; }
        try {
          var f = DriveApp.getFolderById(id);
          row.ok = true; row.url = f.getUrl(); row.folderName = f.getName();
          try {
            var sub = f.getFolders(), months = [];
            while (sub.hasNext() && months.length < 3) months.push(sub.next().getName());
            row.months = months;
          } catch (eS) {}
        } catch (e2) { row.message = "開けません（共有をご確認ください）"; }
        fcOut.push(row);
      });
      return J({ status: "ok", folders: fcOut });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_upload_session") {
      var ses = recCreateUploadSession(d);
      return J(ses.ok ? { status: "ok", result: ses } : { status: "error", message: ses.message });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_upload_complete") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recCompleteUpload(d) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_missing_today") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recMissingToday() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_upload") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recUploadOne(d) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_upload_status") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recUploadStatus(d.pin) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_provision_v81") {
      var pv81 = recProvisionV81(d.rootId || "");
      return J(pv81.ok ? { status: "ok", result: pv81 } : { status: "error", message: pv81.message });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_collect") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recCollectAll(d.days || 7) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_device_setup") {
      return J({ status: "ok", setups: recDeviceSetup_(), rootId: recRootFolder_() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_provision") {
      var pv = provisionRecFolders(d.rootId || "");
      return J(pv.ok ? { status:"ok", root:{id:pv.rootId,url:pv.rootUrl,name:pv.rootName},
                         inboxId:pv.inboxId, inboxUrl:pv.inboxUrl, members:pv.members }
                     : { status:"error", message: pv.message });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_ingest") {
      var pr = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
      return J({ status:"ok", results: recIngestAll(d.days||7,
        d.rootId || pr.getProperty("REC_ROOT_FOLDER") || "",
        d.inboxId || pr.getProperty("REC_INBOX_FOLDER") || "") });
    }
    if (d.type === "nightly_run") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: nightlyPipeline() });
    }
    if (d.type === "nightly_trigger") {
      return J({ status: "ok", trigger: (d.action === "set") ? setNightly(d.on === true, d.hour) : nightlyStatus() });
    }
    if (d.type === "nightly_status") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: nightlyStatus() });
    }
    if (d.type === "kanri_wb_install") {
      var wi;
      try { wi = kanriInstallTemplate(d.rootId || "", d.template || "", d.months || 12); }
      catch (eTpl) { try { wi = k5InstallV3(d.rootId || "", d.months || 12); }
      catch (eV3) { wi = k5InstallV2(d.rootId || "", d.months || 12); } }
      return J(wi.ok ? { status: "ok", result: wi } : { status: "error", message: wi.message, needXlsx: !!wi.needXlsx, folderUrl: wi.folderUrl || "" });
    }
    if (d.type === "kanri_wb_trigger") {
      return J({ status: "ok", trigger: (d.action === "set") ? setKanriWorkbookTrigger(d.on === true, d.hours) : getKanriWorkbookTrigger() });
    }
    if (d.type === "kanri_drive_build") {
      var kb = kanriBuildDriveSystem(d.rootId || KANRI_DRIVE_ROOT, d.months || 6);
      return J(kb.ok ? { status: "ok", result: kb } : { status: "error", message: kb.message });
    }
    if (d.type === "kanri_drive_trigger") {
      return J({ status: "ok", trigger: (d.action === "set") ? setKanriDriveTrigger(d.on === true, d.hours) : getKanriDriveTrigger() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_src_get") {
      var srcList = recSrcRead_(), srcOut = [];
      Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (pn) {
        var f = null;
        srcList.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.pin) === String(pn)) f = x; });
        srcOut.push({ pin: pn, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[pn], mail: personalEmailFor(pn) || "",
          set: !!(f && f.folderId), folderId: f ? f.folderId : "",
          enabled: f ? f.enabled : true, lastAt: f ? f.lastAt : "", lastResult: f ? f.lastResult : "" });
      });
      return J({ status: "ok", configs: srcOut, rootDefault: REC_ROOT_DEFAULT });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_src_set") {
      recSrcWrite_({ pin: d.pin, folderId: d.folderId, enabled: d.enabled });
      return J({ status: "ok" });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_src_autolink") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recAutoLinkShared_() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_pull") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recPullAll(d.rootId || "", d.days || 7) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_macro_check") {
      return J({ status: "ok", result: recMacroCheck(d.pin, d.windowMin || 30) });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_macro_setup") {
      var setups = [];
      Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (pn) { setups.push(recMacroSetup_(pn)); });
      return J({ status: "ok", setups: setups, mailTo: DAILY_REPORT_TO_REC() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_config_get") {
      var list = readRecConfigs_(), out = [];
      Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function (pn) {
        var f = null; list.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.pin) === String(pn)) f = x; });
        out.push({ pin: pn, name: FULL_NAME_MAP[pn], set: !!f, folderId: f ? f.folderId : "",
          tol: f ? f.tol : 5, enabled: f ? f.enabled : true,
          lastScan: f ? f.lastScan : "", lastResult: f ? f.lastResult : "" });
      });
      return J({ status: "ok", configs: out });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_config_set") { writeRecConfig_({pin:d.pin,folderId:d.folderId,tol:d.tol,enabled:d.enabled}); return J({status:"ok"}); }
    if (d.type === "rec_scan") { return J({ status:"ok", reports: recScanAll(d.pin||null, d.days||7) }); }
    if (d.type === "rec_trigger") {
      return J({ status:"ok", trigger: (d.action==="set") ? setRecTrigger(d.on===true, d.hours) : getRecTriggerStatus() });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_list") { return J({ status:"ok", items: recListFor_(d.pin||"", d.month||"", d.result||"", d.limit||60) }); }
    if (d.type === "rec_self") {
      var sh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID).getSheetByName("架電結果");
      var nm = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(d.pin||"")]||"";
      var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Tokyo", "yyyy-MM-dd");
      var calls=0, rec=0, missing=[];
      if (sh && sh.getLastRow()>1) {
        var rows=sh.getDataRange().getValues(), ci=rows[0].indexOf("録音URL");
        for (var i=1;i<rows.length;i++){
          if(String(rows[i][0]||"").trim()!==nm)continue;
          if(normalizeYMD(rows[i][1])!==today)continue;
          calls++;
          if(ci>=0&&String(rows[i][ci]||""))rec++;
          else if(missing.length<5)missing.push(String(rows[i][3]||"")+"("+String(rows[i][2]||"")+")");
        }
      }
      return J({ status:"ok", calls:calls, rec:rec, pct: calls?Math.round(rec/calls*1000)/10:0, missing:missing });
    }
    if (d.type === "rec_status") {
      var ss2=SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID), sh2=ss2.getSheetByName("架電結果");
      var month=String(d.month||""), agg={};
      if (sh2 && sh2.getLastRow()>1) {
        var rw=sh2.getDataRange().getValues(), ui=rw[0].indexOf("録音URL");
        for (var k=1;k<rw.length;k++){
          var ymd=normalizeYMD(rw[k][1]);
          if(!ymd||(month&&ymd.slice(0,7)!==month))continue;
          var n2=String(rw[k][0]||"").trim(); if(!n2)continue;
          if(!agg[n2])agg[n2]={calls:0,rec:0};
          agg[n2].calls++;
          if(ui>=0&&String(rw[k][ui]||""))agg[n2].rec++;
        }
      }
      var members=[];
      Object.keys(FULL_NAME_MAP).forEach(function(pn){
        var nm3=FULL_NAME_MAP[pn], v=agg[nm3]; if(!v)return;
        members.push({pin:pn,name:nm3,calls:v.calls,rec:v.rec,pct:v.calls?Math.round(v.rec/v.calls*1000)/10:0,days:[]});
      });
      return J({ status:"ok", month:month, members:members });
    }
  } catch (e) { return J({ status:"error", message:String(e) }); }
  return null;
}


// ═══ v70: 経営システムへ「録音カバー率」も渡す（品質指標の統合） ═══
// 純粋関数：架電結果から、担当者×日ごとの録音有無を集計する
function kanriRecCoverage_(hist, fromKey) {
  var by = {}, hdr = hist && hist.length ? hist[0] : [];
  var ui = -1;
  for (var c = 0; c < hdr.length; c++) { if (String(hdr[c]).indexOf("録音URL") >= 0) ui = c; }
  for (var i = 1; i < (hist || []).length; i++) {
    var nm = String(hist[i][0] || "").trim();
    var dk = kanriDateKey_(hist[i][1]);
    if (!nm || !dk || dk < fromKey) continue;
    var k = nm + "|" + dk;
    if (!by[k]) by[k] = { name: nm, d: dk, calls: 0, rec: 0 };
    by[k].calls++;
    if (ui >= 0 && String(hist[i][ui] || "")) by[k].rec++;
  }
  var out = [];
  for (var key in by) {
    var v = by[key];
    out.push({ d: v.d, name: v.name, calls: v.calls, rec: v.rec,
      pct: v.calls ? Math.round(v.rec / v.calls * 1000) / 10 : 0 });
  }
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d === b.d ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1); });
  return out;
}
// 経営システムが「いつ時点のデータか」を判断するための鮮度情報
function kanriFreshness_() {
  var last = "";
  try { last = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("KANRI_LAST_LIVE") || ""; } catch (e) {}
  return { lastLive: last, mode: "event+snapshot", serverTime: new Date().toISOString() };
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v71: MacroDroid の導通チェック
//   「マクロを作ったけれど、本当に届いているのか」を確認するための機能。
//   直近◯分のメールを調べ、届いていない場合は原因まで示す。
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// 受信メール一覧から、その担当者の録音が届いているかを判定する（純粋関数＝検証しやすい）
//   mails: [{from, subject, dateMs, attachments:[{name,size}]}]
function recMacroVerdict_(mails, pin, nowMs, windowMin) {
  var win = (Number(windowMin) || 30) * 60000;
  var myMail = String(personalEmailFor(pin) || "").toLowerCase();
  var myName = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(pin)] || "";
  var res = { ok: false, state: "", found: null, issues: [], checked: 0 };

  var recent = (mails || []).filter(function (m) { return (nowMs - m.dateMs) <= win; });
  res.checked = recent.length;

  // ① 自分から届いた「音声つき」のメール＝成功
  for (var i = 0; i < recent.length; i++) {
    var m = recent[i];
    var fromAddr = String(m.from || "").toLowerCase();
    var byEmail = myMail && fromAddr.indexOf(myMail) >= 0;
    var bySubject = myName && String(m.subject || "").indexOf(myName) >= 0;
    if (!byEmail && !bySubject) continue;
    var audio = (m.attachments || []).filter(function (a) { return recAttachmentOk_(a.name, a.size); });
    if (audio.length) {
      res.ok = true; res.state = "成功";
      res.found = { from: m.from, subject: m.subject, dateMs: m.dateMs,
        fileName: audio[0].name, size: audio[0].size, count: audio.length };
      if (!byEmail) res.issues.push("会社のGmail以外から届いています（件名の名前で判別しました）");
      return res;
    }
  }
  // ② 自分から届いているが、音声が付いていない
  for (var j = 0; j < recent.length; j++) {
    var m2 = recent[j];
    var f2 = String(m2.from || "").toLowerCase();
    if (!(myMail && f2.indexOf(myMail) >= 0)) continue;
    var atts = m2.attachments || [];
    res.state = "添付が正しくない";
    if (!atts.length) res.issues.push("メールは届いていますが、添付ファイルがありません（マクロの『添付＝追加されたファイル』を確認）");
    else {
      var big = atts.filter(function (a) { return a.size > REC_MAX_MB * 1024 * 1024; });
      if (big.length) res.issues.push("添付が大きすぎます（" + Math.round(big[0].size / 1048576) + "MB／上限" + REC_MAX_MB + "MB）");
      else res.issues.push("添付が音声ファイルではありません（" + atts[0].name + "）");
    }
    return res;
  }
  // ③ そもそも届いていない
  res.state = "未着";
  res.issues.push("直近" + (Number(windowMin) || 30) + "分に、このメンバーからのメールが届いていません");
  if (!myMail) res.issues.push("この方の会社Gmailが未登録です（件名に名前を入れて送ってもらってください）");
  return res;
}
// 実際にGmailを調べて判定する
function recMacroCheck(pin, windowMin) {
  var win = Number(windowMin) || 30;
  var mails = [];
  try {
    var threads = GmailApp.search("newer_than:1d", 0, 30);
    for (var t = 0; t < threads.length; t++) {
      var ms = threads[t].getMessages();
      for (var i = 0; i < ms.length; i++) {
        var atts = [];
        try {
          var ax = ms[i].getAttachments();
          for (var a = 0; a < ax.length; a++) atts.push({ name: ax[a].getName(), size: ax[a].getSize() });
        } catch (e) {}
        mails.push({ from: ms[i].getFrom(), subject: ms[i].getSubject(),
          dateMs: ms[i].getDate().getTime(), attachments: atts });
      }
    }
  } catch (e2) {
    return { ok: false, state: "確認できません", issues: ["Gmailを読めません: " + e2], checked: 0 };
  }
  var v = recMacroVerdict_(mails, pin, new Date().getTime(), win);
  v.name = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(pin)] || "";
  v.mail = personalEmailFor(pin) || "";
  logAudit("導通チェック", "管理者", v.name, v.state);
  return v;
}
// 端末に入力してもらう設定値を組み立てる（印刷・画面表示に使う）
function recMacroSetup_(pin) {
  var name = FULL_NAME_MAP[String(pin)] || "";
  return {
    pin: String(pin), name: name,
    mailTo: DAILY_REPORT_TO_REC(),
    subject: "録音 " + name,
    folder: "Call",
    fromAccount: personalEmailFor(pin) || "（会社Gmail未登録）",
    steps: [
      "MacroDroid を開き「マクロを追加」",
      "トリガー → ファイル → ファイル追加 → フォルダ「Call」を選ぶ",
      "アクション → メール → メール送信",
      "宛先に " + DAILY_REPORT_TO_REC() + " を入力",
      "件名に「録音 " + name + "」を入力",
      "添付で「トリガーとなったファイル」を選ぶ",
      "マクロ名を「録音自動送信」にして保存 → 有効(ON)にする"
    ]
  };
}
// 録音の送付先（既定は管理者。必要ならスクリプトプロパティ REC_MAIL_TO で変更可）
function DAILY_REPORT_TO_REC() {
  try {
    var v = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("REC_MAIL_TO");
    if (v) return String(v);
  } catch (e) {}
  return "hr.ashikaga@gmail.com";
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v72: Googleドライブ上で完結する経営管理システム
//   指定フォルダの中にフォルダ構成とスプレッドシートを自動生成し、
//   架電システムのデータで自動更新する。
//   → 経営PC・Python・Excel が不要になり、スマホからも見られる。
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var KANRI_DRIVE_ROOT = "1rR9JXEFWlJMwwb8RAoNqkxey5Wri0g0i";  // 経営管理システムを置くドライブフォルダ
var KANRI_SS_NAME = "スキマグループ_経営管理システム";
var KANRI_FOLDERS = ["01_月次レポート", "02_バックアップ", "03_エクスポート"];

// 月キー（yyyy-MM）
function kanriMonthKey_(dateKey) { return String(dateKey || "").slice(0, 7); }

// 日次集計シートに書く行を作る（純粋関数）
function kanriDailyRows_(days) {
  var out = [];
  (days || []).forEach(function (d) {
    var conn = Number(d.connects) || 0, calls = Number(d.calls) || 0, apo = Number(d.appos) || 0;
    out.push([d.d, d.name, (d.hours === null || d.hours === undefined) ? "" : d.hours,
      d.est ? "推定" : "", calls, conn, apo,
      calls ? Math.round(conn / calls * 1000) / 10 : 0,
      calls ? Math.round(apo / calls * 1000) / 10 : 0,
      (d.hours ? Math.round(calls / d.hours * 10) / 10 : "")]);
  });
  return out;
}
// アポインター別の合計（純粋関数）
function kanriMemberRollup_(days) {
  var m = {};
  (days || []).forEach(function (d) {
    if (!m[d.name]) m[d.name] = { name: d.name, days: 0, hours: 0, calls: 0, connects: 0, appos: 0 };
    var r = m[d.name];
    r.days++;
    r.hours += Number(d.hours) || 0;
    r.calls += Number(d.calls) || 0;
    r.connects += Number(d.connects) || 0;
    r.appos += Number(d.appos) || 0;
  });
  var out = [];
  for (var k in m) {
    var v = m[k];
    out.push([v.name, v.days, Math.round(v.hours * 10) / 10, v.calls, v.connects, v.appos,
      v.calls ? Math.round(v.connects / v.calls * 1000) / 10 : 0,
      v.calls ? Math.round(v.appos / v.calls * 1000) / 10 : 0,
      v.hours ? Math.round(v.calls / v.hours * 10) / 10 : 0,
      v.days ? Math.round(v.appos / v.days * 100) / 100 : 0]);
  }
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return b[5] - a[5]; });   // アポ数の多い順
  return out;
}
// 月別の推移（純粋関数）
function kanriMonthlyRollup_(days) {
  var m = {};
  (days || []).forEach(function (d) {
    var mk = kanriMonthKey_(d.d);
    if (!mk) return;
    if (!m[mk]) m[mk] = { mk: mk, dates: {}, hours: 0, calls: 0, connects: 0, appos: 0 };
    var r = m[mk];
    r.dates[d.d] = 1;
    r.hours += Number(d.hours) || 0;
    r.calls += Number(d.calls) || 0;
    r.connects += Number(d.connects) || 0;
    r.appos += Number(d.appos) || 0;
  });
  var out = [];
  for (var k in m) {
    var v = m[k], nd = 0;
    for (var x in v.dates) nd++;
    out.push([v.mk, nd, Math.round(v.hours * 10) / 10, v.calls, v.connects, v.appos,
      v.calls ? Math.round(v.appos / v.calls * 1000) / 10 : 0,
      nd ? Math.round(v.appos / nd * 100) / 100 : 0]);
  }
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : 1; });
  return out;
}
// 子フォルダを用意（無ければ作る）
function kanriChildFolder_(parent, name) {
  var it = parent.getFoldersByName(name);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : parent.createFolder(name);
}
// フォルダ内の指定名スプレッドシートを取得（無ければ作る）
function kanriGetOrCreateSS_(folder, name) {
  var it = folder.getFilesByName(name);
  while (it.hasNext()) {
    var f = it.next();
    if (f.getMimeType() === MimeType.GOOGLE_SHEETS) return SpreadsheetApp.openById(f.getId());
  }
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(name);
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
  folder.addFile(file);
  try { DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch (e) {}
  return ss;
}
// シートを用意して中身を入れ替える
function kanriPutSheet_(ss, name, header, rows, widths) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name);
  sh.clear();
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, header.length).setValues([header]);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, header.length).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e8eaf6");
  if (rows && rows.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, header.length).setValues(rows);
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  (widths || []).forEach(function (w, i) { try { sh.setColumnWidth(i + 1, w); } catch (e) {} });
  return sh;
}

// ★本体：ドライブ内に経営管理システムを構築／更新する
function kanriBuildDriveSystem(rootIdOrUrl, months) {
  var rootId = extractFolderId_(rootIdOrUrl || KANRI_DRIVE_ROOT);
  var root;
  try { root = DriveApp.getFolderById(rootId); }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, message: "フォルダを開けません（共有設定をご確認ください）: " + e }; }

  // フォルダ構成を用意
  var home = kanriChildFolder_(root, "経営管理システム");
  var subs = {};
  KANRI_FOLDERS.forEach(function (n) { subs[n] = kanriChildFolder_(home, n); });

  // データを集計
  var ss0 = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  function vals(nm) { var sh = ss0.getSheetByName(nm); return (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1) ? sh.getDataRange().getValues() : []; }
  var back = (Number(months) || 6) * 31;
  var fromKey = kanriDateKey_(new Date(Date.now() - back * 86400000));
  var att = vals("勤怠"), hist = vals("架電結果"), apo = vals("アポイント"), shift = vals("シフト申請");
  var agg = kanriAggregate_(att, hist, apo, fromKey);
  var q = kanriQuality_(att, hist, apo, fromKey);
  try { kanriQualityV2_(q, att, hist, shift); } catch (eq) {}
  var cover = [];
  try { cover = kanriRecCoverage_(hist, fromKey); } catch (ec) {}

  // スプレッドシートを用意
  var ss = kanriGetOrCreateSS_(home, KANRI_SS_NAME);

  // ① 日次集計
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "日次集計",
    ["日付", "名前", "実働h", "推定", "架電", "接続", "アポ", "接続率%", "アポ率%", "架電/h"],
    kanriDailyRows_(agg.days), [90, 100, 60, 50, 60, 60, 60, 70, 70, 70]);

  // ② アポインター別
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "アポインター別",
    ["名前", "稼働日数", "実働h計", "架電", "接続", "アポ", "接続率%", "アポ率%", "架電/h", "アポ/日"],
    kanriMemberRollup_(agg.days), [110, 70, 70, 60, 60, 60, 70, 70, 70, 70]);

  // ③ 月次推移
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "月次推移",
    ["対象月", "稼働日数", "実働h計", "架電", "接続", "アポ", "アポ率%", "アポ/日"],
    kanriMonthlyRollup_(agg.days), [80, 80, 80, 70, 70, 70, 70, 70]);

  // ④ アポ一覧
  var apoRows = (agg.appos || []).map(function (a) { return [a.md, a.mt, a.project, a.company, a.reporter]; });
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "アポ一覧", ["商談日", "時間", "案件名", "法人名", "報告者"],
    apoRows, [90, 70, 120, 220, 100]);

  // ⑤ 品質レポート
  var qRows = [];
  (q.punchMiss || []).forEach(function (x) { qRows.push(["打刻漏れ", x.name, x.d, x.note || ""]); });
  (q.unknownNames || []).forEach(function (x) { qRows.push(["名簿外の名前", x.name, "", x.rows + "行"]); });
  (q.apoMismatch || []).forEach(function (x) {
    qRows.push(["アポ件数の差異", x.name, x.d,
      "架電結果" + x.histAppo + "件 / 報告" + x.reported + "件" + (x.fix ? "（" + x.fix + "）" : "")]);
  });
  (q.idleWork || []).forEach(function (x) { qRows.push(["稼働空転", x.name, x.d, x.note || "出勤打刻あり・架電0件"]); });
  (q.shiftGap || []).forEach(function (x) { qRows.push(["シフト乖離", x.name, x.month || "", x.note || ""]); });
  (q.apoDrop || []).forEach(function (x) { qRows.push(["アポ率急落", x.name, "", x.note || ""]); });
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "品質レポート", ["種別", "対象者", "日付/月", "内容"], qRows, [110, 100, 90, 420]);

  // ⑥ 録音カバレッジ
  var cRows = (cover || []).map(function (c) { return [c.d, c.name, c.calls, c.rec, c.pct]; });
  kanriPutSheet_(ss, "録音カバレッジ", ["日付", "名前", "架電", "録音", "カバー率%"], cRows, [90, 100, 60, 60, 80]);

  // ⑦ ダッシュボード（数式で自動計算）
  kanriBuildDriveDashboard_(ss);

  // 月次レポートのコピーを残す（当月分を上書き）
  var mk = kanriMonthKey_(kanriDateKey_(new Date()));
  try {
    var mfolder = kanriChildFolder_(subs["01_月次レポート"], mk);
    var copyName = KANRI_SS_NAME + "_" + mk;
    var old = mfolder.getFilesByName(copyName);
    while (old.hasNext()) { old.next().setTrashed(true); }
    DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId()).makeCopy(copyName, mfolder);
  } catch (em) {}

  // CSVエクスポート（外部BI用）
  try {
    var csv = kanriCsv_(agg.days);
    var ex = subs["03_エクスポート"];
    var oldc = ex.getFilesByName("daily.csv");
    while (oldc.hasNext()) { oldc.next().setTrashed(true); }
    ex.createFile("daily.csv", csv, MimeType.CSV);
  } catch (ex2) {}

  try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("KANRI_DRIVE_SS", ss.getId()); } catch (e3) {}
  logAudit("経営システム構築", "管理者", ss.getName(), "日次" + agg.days.length + "行");
  return { ok: true, url: ss.getUrl(), id: ss.getId(), folderUrl: home.getUrl(),
    days: agg.days.length, members: kanriMemberRollup_(agg.days).length,
    quality: qRows.length, coverage: cRows.length };
}

// ダッシュボード（数式ベース＝開くたびに最新の計算結果になる）
function kanriBuildDriveDashboard_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName("ダッシュボード") || ss.insertSheet("ダッシュボード", 0);
  sh.clear();
  var D = "'日次集計'";
  var today = 'TEXT(TODAY(),"yyyy-mm-dd")';
  var month = 'TEXT(TODAY(),"yyyy-mm")';
  var rows = [
    ["スキマグループ 経営管理システム", "", "", ""],
    ["最終更新", '=TEXT(NOW(),"yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm")', "", ""],
    ["", "", "", ""],
    ["■ 本日", "", "■ 今月", ""],
    ["架電", '=SUMIF(' + D + '!A:A,' + today + ',' + D + '!E:E)',
      "架電", '=SUMPRODUCT((LEFT(' + D + '!A2:A10000,7)=' + month + ')*' + D + '!E2:E10000)'],
    ["接続", '=SUMIF(' + D + '!A:A,' + today + ',' + D + '!F:F)',
      "接続", '=SUMPRODUCT((LEFT(' + D + '!A2:A10000,7)=' + month + ')*' + D + '!F2:F10000)'],
    ["アポ", '=SUMIF(' + D + '!A:A,' + today + ',' + D + '!G:G)',
      "アポ", '=SUMPRODUCT((LEFT(' + D + '!A2:A10000,7)=' + month + ')*' + D + '!G2:G10000)'],
    ["実働h", '=SUMIF(' + D + '!A:A,' + today + ',' + D + '!C:C)',
      "実働h", '=SUMPRODUCT((LEFT(' + D + '!A2:A10000,7)=' + month + ')*' + D + '!C2:C10000)'],
    ["アポ率%", '=IF(B5=0,0,ROUND(B7/B5*100,1))', "アポ率%", '=IF(D5=0,0,ROUND(D7/D5*100,1))'],
    ["接続率%", '=IF(B5=0,0,ROUND(B6/B5*100,1))', "接続率%", '=IF(D5=0,0,ROUND(D6/D5*100,1))'],
    ["", "", "", ""],
    ["■ 品質チェック", '=COUNTA(\'品質レポート\'!A2:A)&"件"', "■ 録音カバー率",
      '=IFERROR(ROUND(SUM(\'録音カバレッジ\'!D2:D)/SUM(\'録音カバレッジ\'!C2:C)*100,1)&"%","—")'],
    ["", "", "", ""],
    ["■ アポインター別（アポ数順）", "", "", ""]
  ];
  sh.getRange(1, 1, rows.length, 4).setValues(rows);
  sh.getRange(1, 1).setFontSize(14).setFontWeight("bold");
  sh.getRange(4, 1).setFontWeight("bold"); sh.getRange(4, 3).setFontWeight("bold");
  sh.getRange(12, 1).setFontWeight("bold"); sh.getRange(12, 3).setFontWeight("bold");
  sh.getRange(14, 1).setFontWeight("bold");
  sh.getRange(15, 1).setFormula("=IFERROR(QUERY('アポインター別'!A:J,\"select A,D,F,H,C where A is not null order by F desc limit 15\",1),\"データがありません\")");
  sh.setColumnWidth(1, 190); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 130);
  sh.setColumnWidth(3, 150); sh.setColumnWidth(4, 130);
  try { ss.setActiveSheet(sh); ss.moveActiveSheet(1); } catch (e) {}
  return sh;
}

// 毎日の自動更新（トリガー用）
function kanriDriveRefresh() {
  var r = kanriBuildDriveSystem(KANRI_DRIVE_ROOT, 6);
  if (!r.ok) { try { kanriNotifyFail_("drive_build", r.message); } catch (e) {} }
  return r;
}
function setKanriDriveTrigger(on, hours) {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === "kanriDriveRefresh") ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
  });
  if (!on) { logAudit("経営システム自動更新", "管理者", "停止", ""); return { on: false }; }
  var hh = Number(hours) || 24;
  if (hh >= 24) ScriptApp.newTrigger("kanriDriveRefresh").timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(20).create();
  else ScriptApp.newTrigger("kanriDriveRefresh").timeBased().everyHours(Math.max(1, hh)).create();
  logAudit("経営システム自動更新", "管理者", "開始(" + (hh >= 24 ? "毎日20時" : hh + "時間ごと") + ")", "");
  return { on: true, hours: hh };
}
function getKanriDriveTrigger() {
  var on = false;
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === "kanriDriveRefresh") on = true;
  });
  return { on: on };
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v73: 本物の経営管理システム（21シート）をGoogleドライブ上で動かす
//   ExcelをGoogleスプレッドシートへ変換し、⑪日次アポ取得ログへ
//   架電システムのデータを流し込む。以降は全21シートが自動計算される。
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var KANRI_WB_NAME = "スキマグループ_経営管理システム";
var KANRI_LOG_SHEET = "日次アポ取得ログ";     // ⑪ 入力起点
var KANRI_LOG_FIRST_ROW = 7;                  // データ開始行（B7）
var KANRI_LOG_LAST_ROW = 306;                 // データ終了行（B306）

// ⑪へ書き込む行を作る（純粋関数）: [日付, 担当, 実働h, 架電, 接続, アポ]
// 数式列（C=曜日 / K=月キー）は触らない。B・D〜H のみを書く。
function kanriLogRows_(days, maxRows) {
  var lim = Number(maxRows) || (KANRI_LOG_LAST_ROW - KANRI_LOG_FIRST_ROW + 1);
  var src = (days || []).slice(0);
  src.sort(function (a, b) { return a.d === b.d ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1); });
  if (src.length > lim) src = src.slice(src.length - lim);   // 新しい方を残す
  var out = [];
  src.forEach(function (d) {
    out.push([d.d, d.name, (d.hours === null || d.hours === undefined) ? "" : d.hours,
      Number(d.calls) || 0, Number(d.connects) || 0, Number(d.appos) || 0]);
  });
  return out;
}
// 見出し行から列位置を割り出す（レイアウトが変わっても追随できるように）
function kanriLogColumns_(headerRow) {
  var map = { date: 0, name: 0, hours: 0, calls: 0, connects: 0, appos: 0 };
  for (var i = 0; i < (headerRow || []).length; i++) {
    var t = String(headerRow[i] || "").replace(/\s/g, "");
    var c = i + 1;
    if (!map.date && t.indexOf("日付") >= 0) map.date = c;
    else if (!map.name && (t.indexOf("担当") >= 0)) map.name = c;
    else if (!map.hours && t.indexOf("実働") >= 0) map.hours = c;
    else if (!map.calls && t.indexOf("架電") >= 0) map.calls = c;
    else if (!map.connects && t.indexOf("接続") >= 0) map.connects = c;
    else if (!map.appos && t.indexOf("アポ") >= 0) map.appos = c;
  }
  return map;
}
// 見出し行を探す（先頭20行から）
function kanriFindHeaderRow_(values) {
  for (var r = 0; r < Math.min(20, (values || []).length); r++) {
    var joined = (values[r] || []).map(function (x) { return String(x || ""); }).join("");
    if (joined.indexOf("日付") >= 0 && joined.indexOf("架電") >= 0 && joined.indexOf("アポ") >= 0) return r + 1;
  }
  return 0;
}

// ── ExcelをGoogleスプレッドシートへ変換して指定フォルダに置く ──
function kanriConvertXlsx_(xlsxFileId, folderId, name) {
  var blob = DriveApp.getFileById(xlsxFileId).getBlob();
  var meta = { name: name, parents: [folderId], mimeType: "application/vnd.google-apps.spreadsheet" };
  var boundary = "kanri" + Date.now();
  var payload = Utilities.newBlob(
    "--" + boundary + "\r\nContent-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8\r\n\r\n" +
    JSON.stringify(meta) + "\r\n--" + boundary + "\r\n" +
    "Content-Type: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet\r\n\r\n"
  ).getBytes()
    .concat(blob.getBytes())
    .concat(Utilities.newBlob("\r\n--" + boundary + "--").getBytes());
  var res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(
    "https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=multipart&supportsAllDrives=true",
    { method: "post", contentType: "multipart/related; boundary=" + boundary,
      payload: payload, muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() } });
  var code = res.getResponseCode();
  if (code < 200 || code >= 300) throw new Error("変換に失敗しました(" + code + "): " + res.getContentText().slice(0, 200));
  return JSON.parse(res.getContentText());   // {id, name, ...}
}
// フォルダ内から Excel（.xlsx）を探す
function kanriFindXlsx_(folder) {
  var newest = null, it = folder.getFiles(), guard = 0;
  while (it.hasNext() && guard < 200) {
    guard++;
    var f = it.next();
    var mt = f.getMimeType();
    if (mt !== "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet") continue;
    if (!newest || f.getDateCreated().getTime() > newest.getDateCreated().getTime()) newest = f;
  }
  return newest;
}
// 変換済みのスプレッドシートを探す
function kanriFindWorkbook_(folder) {
  var it = folder.getFilesByType(MimeType.GOOGLE_SHEETS), best = null, guard = 0;
  while (it.hasNext() && guard < 200) {
    guard++;
    var f = it.next();
    if (f.getName().indexOf(KANRI_WB_NAME) !== 0) continue;
    if (!best || f.getDateUpdated().getTime() > best.getDateUpdated().getTime()) best = f;
  }
  return best;
}

// ★設置：フォルダ内のExcelをスプレッドシートに変換し、データを流し込む
function kanriInstallWorkbook(rootIdOrUrl) {
  var rootId = extractFolderId_(rootIdOrUrl || KANRI_DRIVE_ROOT);
  var root;
  try { root = DriveApp.getFolderById(rootId); }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, message: "フォルダを開けません（共有設定をご確認ください）" }; }
  var home = kanriChildFolder_(root, "経営管理システム");

  var wb = kanriFindWorkbook_(home);
  var converted = false;
  if (!wb) {
    var xlsx = kanriFindXlsx_(home) || kanriFindXlsx_(root);
    if (!xlsx) {
      return { ok: false, needXlsx: true, folderUrl: home.getUrl(),
        message: "Excel（スキマグループ_経営管理システム.xlsx）が見つかりません。フォルダに1つ置いてから、もう一度お試しください。" };
    }
    var made = kanriConvertXlsx_(xlsx.getId(), home.getId(), KANRI_WB_NAME);
    wb = DriveApp.getFileById(made.id);
    converted = true;
  }
  var fed = kanriFeedWorkbook_(wb.getId());
  try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("KANRI_WB_ID", wb.getId()); } catch (e2) {}
  logAudit("経営システム設置", "管理者", wb.getName(), (converted ? "変換+" : "") + "投入" + fed.rows + "行");
